--- FILE: src/components/sections/Hero.jsx ---
import { useRef } from 'react'
import { motion, useScroll, useTransform, useSpring } from 'framer-motion'
import { MoveRight } from 'lucide-react'
import MagneticButton from '../ui/MagneticButton'
import { WhatsApp } from '../ui/SocialIcons'
import { heroProducts } from '../../data/content'

const springConfig = { stiffness: 300, damping: 30, bounce: 100 }

function Header() {
  return (
    <div className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 z-20 mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 pt-24 pb-4 opacity-100 md:pt-28 md:pb-6">
      <h1 className="max-w-3xl text-[clamp(2.75rem,7vw,5.5rem)] font-bold leading-[0.98] tracking-[-0.035em] text-fg">
        Código com raiz,
        <br />
        produto que <span className="text-moss">escala</span>.
      </h1>
      <p className="mt-8 max-w-xl text-lg font-medium leading-relaxed text-fg-soft md:text-xl">
        Somos uma software house que projeta, constrói e escala produtos digitais. Do primeiro
        rascunho ao lançamento, com equipe qualificada e código que continua sendo seu.
      </p>
      <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row">
        <MagneticButton
          onClick={() => document.querySelector('#contato')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' })}
          className="btn-ghost-light px-8 py-4 text-base"
        >
          Agendar conversa
          <WhatsApp className="h-5 w-5" />
        </MagneticButton>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => document.querySelector('#trabalho')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' })}
          className="btn-lime px-8 py-4 text-base"
        >
          Ver nosso trabalho
          <MoveRight className="h-5 w-5" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function ProductCard({ product, translate, lazy }) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      style={{ x: translate }}
      whileHover={{ y: -20 }}
      className="group/product relative h-96 w-[30rem] shrink-0"
    >
      <a href={product.link} className="block group-hover/product:shadow-2xl">
        <img
          src={product.thumbnail}
          alt={product.title}
          width={480}
          height={384}
          decoding="async"
          loading={lazy ? 'lazy' : 'eager'}
          className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover object-left-top"
        />
      </a>
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-forest opacity-0 group-hover/product:opacity-80" />
      <h2 className="absolute bottom-4 left-4 text-cream opacity-0 group-hover/product:opacity-100">
        {product.title}
      </h2>
    </motion.div>
  )
}

export default function Hero() {
  const firstRow = heroProducts.slice(0, 5)
  const secondRow = heroProducts.slice(5, 10)
  const ref = useRef(null)
  const { scrollYProgress } = useScroll({
    target: ref,
    offset: ['start start', 'end start'],
  })

  const translateX = useSpring(useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 1], [0, 1000]), springConfig)
  const translateXReverse = useSpring(useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 1], [0, -1000]), springConfig)
  const rotateX = useSpring(useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.2], [15, 0]), springConfig)
  const opacity = useSpring(useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.2], [0.45, 1]), springConfig)
  const rotateZ = useSpring(useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.2], [20, 0]), springConfig)
  const translateY = useSpring(useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.22], [-40, 520]), springConfig)
  const veil = useSpring(useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.12], [1, 0]), springConfig)

  return (
    <section
      id="top"
      ref={ref}
      className="relative flex h-[160vh] flex-col overflow-hidden bg-canvas pt-8 pb-0 antialiased [perspective:1000px] [transform-style:preserve-3d]"
    >
      <Header />
      <motion.div
        className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-[15] bg-gradient-to-r from-canvas via-canvas/80 to-transparent"
        style={{ opacity: veil }}
      />
      <motion.div className="relative z-10 mt-40" style={{ rotateX, rotateZ, translateY, opacity }}>
        <motion.div className="mb-12 flex flex-row-reverse space-x-20 space-x-reverse">
          {firstRow.map((product) => (
            <ProductCard product={product} translate={translateX} key={product.title} />
          ))}
        </motion.div>
        <motion.div className="flex flex-row space-x-20">
          {secondRow.map((product) => (
            <ProductCard product={product} translate={translateXReverse} key={product.title} lazy />
          ))}
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </section>
  )
}

--- FILE: src/components/sections/TestimonialsCarousel.jsx ---
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react'
import { testimonials } from '../../data/content'
import ScrollReveal from '../ui/ScrollReveal'

export default function TestimonialsCarousel() {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0)

  useEffect(() => {
    const tick = () => {
      if (document.hidden) return
      setIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % testimonials.length)
    }
    const timer = setInterval(tick, 6000)
    return () => clearInterval(timer)
  }, [])

  const next = () => setIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % testimonials.length)
  const prev = () => setIndex((prev) => (prev - 1 + testimonials.length) % testimonials.length)

  return (
    <section id="depoimentos" className="relative overflow-hidden bg-canvas py-28 md:py-36">
      <div className="container-page relative">
        <ScrollReveal className="mb-12 text-center">
          <p className="eyebrow eyebrow-coral">O que dizem</p>
        </ScrollReveal>

        <div className="relative mx-auto flex min-h-[320px] max-w-4xl flex-col justify-center">
          <span
            className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-24 left-0 select-none font-display text-[14rem] leading-none text-coral/10 md:text-[20rem]"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            &ldquo;
          </span>

          <div className="relative z-10 text-center" data-cursor="text">
            <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
              <motion.figure
                key={index}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -24 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: [0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94] }}
                className="flex flex-col items-center"
              >
                <blockquote className="font-display text-[clamp(1.6rem,4vw,3rem)] font-medium leading-[1.2] tracking-[-0.015em] text-fg">
                  {testimonials[index].quote}
                </blockquote>
                <figcaption className="mt-10">
                  <p className="font-condensed text-sm uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-coral">
                    {testimonials[index].author}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-fg-soft">{testimonials[index].company}</p>
                </figcaption>
              </motion.figure>
            </AnimatePresence>
          </div>

          <div className="relative z-10 mt-14 flex flex-col items-center justify-between gap-6 sm:flex-row">
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={prev}
                aria-label="Depoimento anterior"
                className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-fg/25 text-fg transition-colors hover:border-fg hover:bg-solid hover:text-solid-fg"
              >
                <ChevronLeft size={20} />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={next}
                aria-label="Próximo depoimento"
                className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-fg/25 text-fg transition-colors hover:border-fg hover:bg-solid hover:text-solid-fg"
              >
                <ChevronRight size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {testimonials.map((_, i) => (
                <button
                  key={i}
                  onClick={() => setIndex(i)}
                  aria-label={`Ir para depoimento ${i + 1}`}
                  className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                    i === index ? 'w-8 bg-coral' : 'w-2 bg-fg/20 hover:bg-fg/40'
                  }`}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

--- FILE: src/components/sections/CaseStudies.jsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { motion, useInView, useMotionValue, useSpring, useTransform } from 'framer-motion'
import { ArrowUpRight } from 'lucide-react'
import { caseStudies } from '../../data/content'
import ScrollReveal from '../ui/ScrollReveal'

const CARD_RATIO = 'aspect-[16/9]'

function CaseCard({ data }) {
  const ref = useRef(null)
  const x = useMotionValue(0)
  const y = useMotionValue(0)
  const springX = useSpring(x, { stiffness: 250, damping: 28 })
  const springY = useSpring(y, { stiffness: 250, damping: 28 })
  const rotateX = useTransform(springY, [-0.5, 0.5], ['5deg', '-5deg'])
  const rotateY = useTransform(springX, [-0.5, 0.5], ['-5deg', '5deg'])
  const [slide, setSlide] = useState(0)
  const images = data.card ? [data.card] : data.images || []
  const inView = useInView(ref, { amount: 0.2 })

  useEffect(() => {
    if (images.length < 2 || !inView) return
    const id = setInterval(() => setSlide((s) => (s + 1) % images.length), 5000)
    return () => clearInterval(id)
  }, [images.length, inView])

  const onMove = (e) => {
    if (!ref.current) return
    const rect = ref.current.getBoundingClientRect()
    x.set((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width - 0.5)
    y.set((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height - 0.5)
  }

  const onLeave = () => {
    x.set(0)
    y.set(0)
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ perspective: 1400 }}>
      <motion.article
        ref={ref}
        onMouseMove={onMove}
        onMouseLeave={onLeave}
        style={{ rotateX, rotateY, transformStyle: 'preserve-3d' }}
        className="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-frame border-2 border-line bg-surface"
        data-cursor="view"
      >
        <div className={`relative ${CARD_RATIO} overflow-hidden bg-canvas-deep`}>
          {images.map((src, i) => (
            <img
              key={src}
              src={src}
              alt=""
              width={800}
              height={450}
              decoding="async"
              loading="lazy"
              className={`absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-contain transition-opacity duration-700 ${
                i === slide ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0'
              }`}
            />
          ))}
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-end p-7">
            <span className="rounded-full border-2 border-fg/20 bg-canvas/80 px-3 py-1 font-condensed text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-fg backdrop-blur-sm">
              {data.industry}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-4 p-7">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">{data.company}</h3>
            <p className="mt-1 font-display text-lg italic text-moss">{data.result}</p>
          </div>
          <span className="text-fg transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:-translate-y-1 group-hover:translate-x-1">
            <ArrowUpRight size={24} />
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="absolute inset-0 translate-y-full border-t-2 border-coral bg-forest p-7 text-cream transition-transform duration-500 ease-out group-hover:translate-y-0">
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-center">
            <p className="font-display text-xl italic text-lime md:text-2xl">{data.result}</p>
            <p className="mt-5 max-w-md leading-relaxed text-cream/85">{data.desc}</p>
            <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {data.services.map((tag) => (
                <span
                  key={tag}
                  className="rounded-full border border-cream/25 px-3 py-1 font-condensed text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-cream/80"
                >
                  {tag}
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </motion.article>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function CaseStudies() {
  return (
    <section id="trabalho" className="bg-canvas py-28 md:py-36">
      <div className="container-page">
        <ScrollReveal className="mb-16 flex flex-col justify-between gap-8 md:flex-row md:items-end">
          <div className="max-w-2xl">
            <p className="eyebrow eyebrow-coral mb-6">Trabalho selecionado</p>
            <h2 className="text-[clamp(2.25rem,5vw,4.25rem)] font-semibold leading-[1.02] tracking-[-0.02em]">
              Resultado que dá para medir.
            </h2>
          </div>
          <a
            href="/portfolio"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2.5 rounded-card border-2 border-fg bg-canvas px-8 py-4 text-base font-bold text-fg transition-colors duration-[400ms] hover:bg-fg hover:text-canvas"
          >
            Mais projetos
            <ArrowUpRight size={20} />
          </a>
        </ScrollReveal>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 md:gap-8">
          {caseStudies.filter((c) => c.home).map((cs, i) => (
            <ScrollReveal key={cs.id} delay={(i % 2) * 0.12} y={54}>
              <CaseCard data={cs} />
            </ScrollReveal>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}


--- FILE: src/components/sections/FAQSection.jsx ---
import * as Accordion from '@radix-ui/react-accordion'
import { Plus, Minus } from 'lucide-react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { faqs } from '../../data/content'
import ScrollReveal from '../ui/ScrollReveal'

export default function FAQSection() {
  const [value, setValue] = useState('')

  return (
    <section className="bg-ink-950 py-32 border-b border-white/5" id="faq">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-6 md:px-12">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-16 lg:gap-24 items-start">
          
          <ScrollReveal>
            <div className="sticky top-32">
              <h2 className="font-display text-[8rem] md:text-[10rem] text-white/5 leading-none font-bold select-none pointer-events-none mb-4 -ml-4 tracking-tighter">
                FAQ
              </h2>
              <div className="relative -mt-16 md:-mt-20 ml-2">
                <p className="font-mono text-xs text-signal uppercase tracking-widest mb-4">The details</p>
                <h3 className="font-display text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold mb-6">Answers to your questions.</h3>
                <p className="text-mist-900 font-body text-lg leading-relaxed max-w-sm">
                  Everything you need to know about how we work, what we charge, and what happens when things go wrong.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </ScrollReveal>

          <ScrollReveal delay={0.2} className="relative z-10 w-full mt-10 lg:mt-32">
            <Accordion.Root 
              type="single" 
              collapsible 
              className="w-full flex flex-col"
              value={value}
              onValueChange={setValue}
            >
              {faqs.map((faq, index) => (
                <Accordion.Item 
                  key={index} 
                  value={`item-${index}`}
                  className="border-b border-white/5 overflow-hidden"
                >
                  <Accordion.Header className="flex">
                    <Accordion.Trigger 
                      className="group font-body text-lg md:text-xl py-6 md:py-8 flex justify-between items-center w-full text-left focus:outline-none transition-colors"
                      data-cursor="hover"
                    >
                      <span className={`tracking-tight pr-8 transition-colors duration-300 ${value === `item-${index}` ? 'text-signal' : 'text-mist-100 group-hover:text-white'}`}>
                        {faq.q}
                      </span>
                      <div className="shrink-0 text-mist-700 transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:text-white">
                        {value === `item-${index}` ? <Minus size={20} className="text-signal"/> : <Plus size={20} />}
                      </div>
                    </Accordion.Trigger>
                  </Accordion.Header>
                  <Accordion.Content asChild forceMount>
                    <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
                      {value === `item-${index}` && (
                        <motion.div
                          initial="collapsed"
                          animate="open"
                          exit="collapsed"
                          variants={{
                            open: { opacity: 1, height: 'auto', marginBottom: 24, marginTop: -8 },
                            collapsed: { opacity: 0, height: 0, marginBottom: 0, marginTop: 0 }
                          }}
                          transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: [0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94] }}
                          className="overflow-hidden"
                        >
                          <p className="text-mist-700 text-sm md:text-base leading-relaxed pr-8">
                            {faq.a}
                          </p>
                        </motion.div>
                      )}
                    </AnimatePresence>
                  </Accordion.Content>
                </Accordion.Item>
              ))}
            </Accordion.Root>
          </ScrollReveal>

        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}


--- FILE: src/components/sections/ProcessTimeline.jsx ---
import { useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { motion, useMotionValueEvent, useScroll } from 'framer-motion'
import { processSteps } from '../../data/content'
import Doodle from '../ui/Doodle'

function Step({ step, index, active, dotRef }) {
  return (
    <div className="relative flex min-h-[52vh] items-center py-10">
      <span
        ref={dotRef}
        className={`absolute left-4 z-10 h-4 w-4 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full border-2 transition-[color,background-color,border-color,transform] duration-500 md:left-6 ${
          active
            ? 'scale-110 border-lime bg-lime'
            : 'border-cream/35 bg-cream/20'
        }`}
      />

      <div className="relative w-full max-w-2xl pl-14 md:pl-24">
        <span
          className={`pointer-events-none absolute -top-10 left-12 font-display text-[6rem] font-bold leading-none md:-top-16 md:left-20 md:text-[10rem] ${
            active ? 'text-lime/20' : 'text-cream/10'
          }`}
        >
          0{index + 1}
        </span>

        <div className="relative z-10">
          <span
            className={`inline-block rounded-full border-2 px-3 py-1 font-condensed text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] ${
              active ? 'border-lime bg-lime text-ink' : 'border-cream/40 text-cream'
            }`}
          >
            {step.duration}
          </span>
          <h3
            className={`mt-5 text-[clamp(2rem,4.5vw,3.25rem)] font-bold leading-[1.05] ${
              active ? 'text-lime' : 'text-cream'
            }`}
          >
            {step.title}
          </h3>
          <p
            className={`mt-3 font-display text-xl font-semibold md:text-2xl ${
              active ? 'text-cream' : 'text-lime/70'
            }`}
          >
            {step.tagline}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-5 max-w-lg leading-relaxed text-cream/90">{step.desc}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function ProcessTimeline() {
  const trackRef = useRef(null)
  const railRef = useRef(null)
  const dotRefs = useRef([])
  const [rail, setRail] = useState({ top: 0, height: 0 })
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0)

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const measure = () => {
      const track = trackRef.current
      if (!track) return
      const dots = dotRefs.current.filter(Boolean)
      if (dots.length < 2) return
      const trackRect = track.getBoundingClientRect()
      const first = dots[0].getBoundingClientRect()
      const last = dots[dots.length - 1].getBoundingClientRect()
      const firstCenter = first.top - trackRect.top + first.height / 2
      const lastCenter = last.top - trackRect.top + last.height / 2
      setRail({ top: firstCenter, height: Math.max(0, lastCenter - firstCenter) })
    }

    measure()
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(measure)
    if (trackRef.current) ro.observe(trackRef.current)
    window.addEventListener('resize', measure)
    return () => {
      ro.disconnect()
      window.removeEventListener('resize', measure)
    }
  }, [])

  const { scrollYProgress } = useScroll({
    target: railRef,
    offset: ['start 0.55', 'end 0.55'],
  })

  useMotionValueEvent(scrollYProgress, 'change', (v) => {
    const n = processSteps.length
    const next = n < 2 ? 0 : Math.min(n - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(v * (n - 1) + 1e-4)))
    setActiveIndex((prev) => (prev === next ? prev : next))
  })

  return (
    <section id="processo" className="stage-forest grain relative overflow-hidden py-28 md:py-36">
      <Doodle
        variant="spiral"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute right-[6%] top-24 hidden h-14 w-14 text-coral/60 lg:block"
      />

      <div className="container-page relative z-10">
        <p className="eyebrow eyebrow-coral mb-6 text-cream">Como trabalhamos</p>
        <h2 className="text-[clamp(2.25rem,5vw,4.25rem)] font-semibold leading-[1.02] tracking-[-0.02em] text-cream">
          Um caminho claro,
          <br />
          sem caixa-preta.
        </h2>
      </div>

      <div ref={trackRef} className="container-page relative mt-12">
        <div
          ref={railRef}
          className="absolute left-4 w-[3px] -translate-x-1/2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-cream/15 md:left-6"
          style={{ top: rail.top, height: rail.height }}
        >
          <motion.div
            style={{ scaleY: scrollYProgress }}
            className="h-full w-full origin-top bg-lime"
          />
        </div>

        {processSteps.map((step, index) => (
          <Step
            key={step.title}
            step={step}
            index={index}
            active={index <= activeIndex}
            dotRef={(el) => {
              dotRefs.current[index] = el
            }}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}
